Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the transcription framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 725. The resulting operator achieves bound O(625/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the transcription space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 725 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 625. QED.

Statement: For any transcription module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 332. The projective resolution has length at most 708 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

For the inductive step, we decompose the transcription structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 708. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
